### [108] [Comparative Study of Multi-Agent Actor-Critic Algorithms in Parameterized Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19117)
*Ubayd Ali Bapoo,Clement N Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于共享经验的多智能体参数化动作强化学习方法，将GAC、SAC、TQC扩展为MAGAC、MASAC、MATQC，实验表明MAGAC性能提升显著，而MASAC和MATQC改进有限，且智能体数量超过五个后性能增益递减但计算成本大幅增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动作强化学习在单智能体场景中已取得良好效果，但向多智能体场景的扩展尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究单智能体算法在多智能体环境中的表现。

Method: 采用共享经验的多智能体框架：多个独立Actor-Critic智能体共享同一个回放缓冲区，但各自维护独立的策略网络和价值网络，不采用CTDE范式。在Platform-v0和Goal-v0基准上对比三种智能体配置（3、5、10个智能体），使用平均评估回报和训练时间作为指标，并采用单因素方差分析和Tukey HSD事后检验进行统计分析。

Result: 多智能体框架对Greedy Actor-Critic的性能提升显著，而MASAC和MATQC相比单智能体版本的改进较为温和。当智能体数量超过五个后，性能提升有限但计算成本大幅上升，MAGAC的计算开销尤其明显。

Conclusion: 揭示了共享经验多智能体Actor-Critic方法在参数化动作强化学习中的性能与计算效率之间的权衡，为算法可扩展性提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Parameterized action reinforcement learning has shown strong performance in environments requiring both discrete action selection and continuous parameterization. Prior work established the effectiveness of single-agent actor-critic algorithms - Greedy Actor-Critic (GAC), Soft Actor-Critic (SAC), and Truncated Quantile Critics (TQC) - on benchmark parameterized action tasks, but their extension to multi-agent settings remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a comparative study of shared-experience multi-agent extensions of these algorithms: Multi-Agent Greedy Actor-Critic (MAGAC), Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC), and Multi-Agent Truncated Quantile Critics (MATQC). Rather than following the centralized training, decentralized execution (CTDE) paradigm, the proposed framework uses multiple independent actor-critic agents that share a replay buffer while maintaining separate policy and value networks. We evaluate the algorithms on the Platform-v0 and Goal-v0 benchmarks against their single-agent counterparts, using three-, five-, and ten-agent configurations to assess scalability. Performance is measured by average evaluation return and training time across ten independent runs, with one-way ANOVA and Tukey HSD post-hoc tests used to assess statistical significance. Results show that the multi-agent framework consistently improves Greedy Actor-Critic performance, while MASAC and MATQC show comparatively modest gains over their single-agent versions. Increasing the number of agents beyond five yields limited additional performance while substantially raising computational cost, particularly for MAGAC. These results highlight a trade-off between learning performance and computational efficiency, offering insight into the scalability of shared-experience multi-agent actor-critic methods for parameterized action reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [109] [Sequential Learner Modeling Using Multi-Relational Graph Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19253)
*Rawaa Alatrash,Mohamed Amine Chatti,Hong Yang,Yumeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MR-ConceptGCN，一种基于多关系图卷积网络和预训练语言模型的无监督概念级序列学习者建模方法，通过融合个人知识图谱、MR-GCN和SBERT来增强语义表征，并在在线用户研究中（n=31）验证了其在教育推荐系统中在准确性、有用性、多样性和满意度等方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的用户建模方法存在两个主要局限：（1）将图中的不同关系类型视为同质的，无法捕获丰富的语义信息；（2）忽略了用户交互序列。多关系GNN虽已在表示学习和推荐中应用，但在用户建模领域尚未被探索。

Method: MR-ConceptGCN结合个人知识图谱(PKG)、多关系图卷积网络(MR-GCN)和预训练语言模型SBERT，获得增强的关系感知和语义感知的知识项表示；再基于学习者未理解的知识概念的嵌入，构建融合长期和短期交互的序列学习者模型。

Result: 通过在线用户研究（n=31）验证，MR-ConceptGCN在教育推荐系统中在准确性、有用性、多样性和满意度等用户关键指标上表现优越。

Conclusion: MR-ConceptGCN通过多关系建模和序列融合有效提升了用户建模效果，为教育领域的个性化推荐提供了新思路。

Abstract: User modeling is a critical task in a variety of personalized systems. Recognizing their effectiveness in learning from graph-structured data, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), particularly Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), are increasingly employed for user modeling. However, existing approaches typically treat different relation types in a graph as homogeneous, limiting their ability to capture richer semantics and construct more informative user models. While multi-relational GNNs (MR-GNNs) have been adopted for representation learning and recommendation, their application for user modeling remains unexplored. Moreover, existing GNN-based user modeling approaches ignore the user interaction sequence. To address these research gaps, in this work we propose MR-ConceptGCN, a novel fully unsupervised approach focused on concept-based sequential learner modeling using multi-relational GCNs (MR-GCNs). MR-ConceptGCN effecively combines Personal Knowledge Graphs (PKGs), MR-GCNs, and the pre-trained language model SBERT to obtain enhanced relation- and semantic-aware representations of the PKG items. The enriched embeddings of the knowledge concepts that a learner did not understand when interacting with learning materials in CourseMapper are then used to construct a sequential learner model that combines long-term and short-term learner interactions. We report the results of an online user study (n = 31), demonstrating the benefits of MR-ConceptGCN in terms of several important user-centric aspects including accuracy, usefulness, diversity, and satisfaction with an educational recommender system.

</details>


### [110] [BioSecBench-Surveillance: A Verifiable Benchmark for AI Agents in Pathogen Genomic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19262)
*Harmon Bhasin,Kevin Flyangolts,Dianzhuo Wang,Evan Seeyave,Arjun Banerjee,Amanda Darling,Joshua Stallings,David Stern,Shawn Higdon,Claire Duvallet,Bryan Tegomoh,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了BioSecBench-Surveillance基准，包含100个评估任务，测试AI代理是否能像人类分析师一样，仅凭原始测序数据和监测背景推断出正确的分析流程。最强的Opus 4.8+PI配置也仅通过约50%任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病原体基因组监测的瓶颈正从数据生成转向数据分析，需要评估AI代理是否能在无人工干预的情况下，可靠地执行基因组监测分析流程。

Method: 构建了包含100个评估任务的基准，涵盖7大类别（从分类学分类到基因工程检测），涉及多样本类型和测序技术。每个任务只提供人类分析师能获得的数据和背景，并以确定性方式评分结构化答案。对16个模型-工具组合进行了3,962次可评分尝试。

Result: 最强配置（Opus 4.8+PI）通过率50.2%（95%CI: 40.1-60.3%），与GPT-5.5+Codex持平。即使代理调用了正确的工作流，错误仍源于参考序列、阈值、过滤器和归一化等参数选择。

Conclusion: BioSecBench-Surveillance为衡量AI代理能否在下一次疫情爆发时被信任执行基因组监测提供了标准化评估基准，当前最优模型仅达到约50%通过率，显示出该领域仍有显著提升空间。

Abstract: As pathogen genomic surveillance scales, the bottleneck is shifting from data generation to analysis. We present BioSecBench-Surveillance, a verifiable benchmark of 100 evaluations testing whether AI agents can infer the right analysis pipeline from raw sequencing data and surveillance context. Each evaluation gives an agent only the data and context a human analyst would have, then grades its structured answer deterministically. The tasks span seven categories, from taxonomic classification to genetic-engineering detection, across diverse sample types and sequencing technologies. Across 3,962 gradable attempts from sixteen model-harness pairs, the strongest configuration cleared only about half. Opus 4.8 with PI led at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.1 to 60.3 percent across 83 evaluations, tied with GPT-5.5 with Codex at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.8 to 59.6 percent, followed by Opus 4.7 with PI at 49.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.0 to 59.2 percent, and Sonnet 4.6 with PI at 48.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 38.9 to 58.3 percent. Even when agents invoked the correct workflows, their mistakes came from the choices around them, such as which references, thresholds, filters, and normalization to apply. BioSecBench-Surveillance provides a standard for measuring whether agents can be trusted to perform genomic surveillance when the next outbreak arrives.

</details>


### [111] [Graph-Based Agentic AI with LangGraph: Workflow Pathways for Long-Running Stateful Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19297)
*Daniel Pearson,Sidney Shapiro,Emiliano Sebastian Gonzalez Venegas,Sanad Al-Khatib,Aurora Pinzón Arzola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向实践者的指南，介绍了基于图的工���流路径（LangGraph）在长时间运行、有状态、多步骤生成式AI业务系统中的应用。通过三个可执行示例（SQL分析与修复循环、带证据门控的智能体检索增强生成、带中断与检查点恢复的人工审核策略流程），展示了类型化状态、条件路由、确定性工具、重试、中断、检查点和追踪的整合方式。文章还明确了LangGraph的适用边界：复杂工作流场景值得使用，而简单的ReAct或SDK循环、结构化提取工具、DSPy优化等更适合其他场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI在业务流程中的应用日益复杂，需要长时间运行、多步骤、有状态的系统，但缺乏针对LangGraph这类低阶编排框架的实践指南。作者希望填补这一空白，帮助从业者理解何时以及如何使用基于图的工作流取代简单的提示逻辑。

Method: 通过三个可执行的配方示例（SQL分析修复循环、智能体RAG证据门控、人工审核中断恢复）来演示LangGraph的关键机制（类型化状态、条件路由、确定性工具、重试、中断、检查点、追踪），并对比分析不同工作流复杂度下的框架选择。

Result: 展示了LangGraph在复杂业务工作流中的实际应用模式，明确了其优势在于显式化路由、暂停和审计追踪，而非将逻辑隐藏在提示中。同时给出了框架选型建议：简单工具调用用ReAct/SDK，结构化提取用模式优先工具，提示优化用DSPy。

Conclusion: LangGraph适用于需要显式工作流结构的状态化多步骤业务系统，应根据工作流复杂度而非盲目选用。关键实践是将路由、暂停和审计线索设计为显式的产品行为，而非隐式的提示逻辑。

Abstract: This paper is a practitioner guide to graph-based workflow pathways for long-running, stateful, multi-step generative AI systems in business processes. Rather than treating LangGraph, a low-level orchestration framework for stateful agents, as a model-quality benchmark target, we present three executable recipes -- SQL analytics with repair loops, agentic retrieval-augmented generation with evidence gating, and human-in-the-loop policy review with interrupt and checkpoint recovery -- to show how typed state, conditional routing, deterministic tools, retries, interrupts, checkpoints, and traces fit together. LangGraph is positioned by workflow-complexity fit, not as a universal default: simpler ReAct-style or plain SDK loops may be better for basic tool use, schema-first tools for structured extraction and validation, and DSPy when prompt or program optimization is the main goal. Each recipe explains when LangGraph is worth the extra structure and which implementation patterns make routes, pauses, and audit trails explicit product behavior rather than hidden prompt logic.

</details>


### [112] [LLM Detection as an Intervention: Downstream Impact under Strategic User Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19300)
*Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Jon Kleinberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了不完美的LLM检测器如何通过改变用户使用LLM的激励，导致LLM使用量和输出质量出现反直觉的变化，包括用户增加LLM使用量和产出更低质量输出的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的广泛采用，检测LLM生成内容的需求日益增长，但现有检测工具并非完美。作者希望探究不完美检测器作为干预手段时，对下游指标（如LLM使用量和输出质量）产生的意外影响。

Method: 构建了一个风格化模型，捕捉用户策略性地选择LLM使用程度以及如何后处理内容以降低被检测属性。通过该模型进行理论分析，并在arXiv摘要的词频数据上进行实证验证。

Result: 1) LLM检测可能反直觉地导致人类增加LLM使用量；2) 即使降低检测属性能提升输出质量，引入检测器仍可能使用户产出更低质量输出；3) 检测器使被检测属性呈现"先升后降"的清晰模式，该模式在arXiv摘要词频数据中得到实证复现。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM检测会扭曲LLM使用量和输出质量，揭示了当检测器作为下游指标干预手段时存在的失败模式，为部署LLM检测工具提供了重要的警示。

Abstract: As LLM adoption becomes more widespread, there is a growing interest in detecting LLM-generated content, for example through LLM detection tools and through heuristics based on language patterns. Detectors operate as an intervention that steers not only the detected attribute itself, but also downstream metrics such as LLM usage and output quality. In this work, we demonstrate how imperfect LLM detectors lead to counterintuitive impacts on these downstream metrics, by distorting how users are incentivized to use LLMs in their workflow. We develop a stylized model which captures how users strategically choose how much to use the LLM and how to post-process content to reduce the detected attribute. Using this model, we show that LLM detection can counterintuitively lead humans to increase their LLM usage. Moreover, even when reducing the detected attribute improves output quality, we find that introducing an LLM detector can lead users to produce lower quality outputs. In contrast, we show that detectors result in a clean "rise-then-fall" pattern for the detected attribute, which we empirically reproduce for word frequencies on arXiv abstracts. Altogether, our work illustrates how LLM detection can distort LLM usage and output quality, uncovering failure modes when LLM detectors operate as an intervention on these downstream metrics.

</details>


### [113] [ResearchArena: Evaluating Sabotage and Monitoring in Automated AI R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19321)
*Lena Libon,Ben Rank,Jehyeok Yeon,David Schmotz,Jeremy Qin,Daniel Donnelly,Derck Prinzhorn,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI控制方法应对自动化AI研发中的安全风险，通过ResearchArena框架评估四种监控策略在检测隐蔽破坏（嵌入型与独立型）上的效果，发现训练数据中的破坏最难检测，仅靠监控执行实验仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体开始自动化AI研发，需要评估其输出是否安全部署，传统信任方式不可靠，因而提出将AI视为潜在 adversary 并使用监控检测隐蔽破坏的控制方法。

Method: 构建ResearchArena框架，涵盖安全后训练、能力后训练、CUDA内核优化和推理服务器优化四个长时域任务；每个任务配有两种隐蔽侧任务（嵌入型破坏和独立型破坏）；评估前沿智能体在破坏与监控两方面的表现，监控类型沿两个轴变化（是否执行并探测产物、是否查看智能体思维链）。

Result: 训练数据中隐藏的破坏最难捕获，标记率不足一半；允许监控在产物上运行实验（而非仅读取轨迹）有帮助但不够；监控仍因仅检查表面、注意到异常但忽略、或使用错误测试探测而遗漏嵌入破坏。

Conclusion: 现有监控方案不足以可靠检测自动化AI研发中的隐蔽破坏，ResearchArena作为模块化框架可用于评估自动化AI研发中的破坏与控制。

Abstract: As AI agents begin to automate AI R&D, we need ways to assess whether their outputs are safe to deploy, even when the agents themselves may be untrusted. AI control offers one such approach: rather than trusting the agent, it treats it as a potential adversary and uses a monitor to detect covert sabotage before deployment. We evaluate AI control for automated AI R&D with ResearchArena, a framework spanning four long-horizon tasks: safety post-training, capabilities post-training, CUDA-kernel optimization, and inference-server optimization. Because the deliverable in AI R&D is an artifact that will be deployed and run, we pair each main task with two kinds of hidden side task: an embedded side task that sabotages the submitted model, adapter, kernel, or server itself, and an independent side task that takes a covert out-of-scope action in the sandbox while leaving the artifact honest. We evaluate frontier agents at both sabotage and monitoring, across four monitor types that vary along two axes: whether the monitor may execute and probe the produced artifact, and whether it sees the agent's chain-of-thought. We find that sabotage hidden in the training data is the hardest to catch, flagged fewer than half the time. Letting the monitor run experiments on the artifact, rather than only read the trajectory, helps, but it is not enough: monitors still miss embedded sabotage by inspecting only the surface, by noticing the anomaly but explaining it away, or by probing the artifact with the wrong test. We release ResearchArena as a modular framework for evaluating sabotage and control in automated AI R&D.

</details>


### [114] [Associative Emotional Learning in Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19327)
*Seowung Leem,Andreas Keil,Mingzhou Ding,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深度神经网络模型，用于模拟视觉效价处理中的联想情绪学习。该模型包含视觉编码模块和情绪效价识别模块，在巴甫洛夫学习范式下成功再现了人类联想学习中的关联形成、泛化以及神经表征对齐等关键现象，并通过与人类实验数据的比较验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算模型（如Rescorla-Wagner模型）在解释联想情绪学习的神经机制方面存在局限性，亟需新的建模方法以更好地理解行为与神经层面的学习过程。

Method: 构建了一个深度神经网络模型，包含处理复杂自然场景的视觉模块和识别情绪效价的模块；设计了新的巴甫洛夫学习范式对模型进行训练和测试。

Result: 模型成功复现了人类联想学习中的关联形成与泛化现象；条件刺激与非条件刺激的神经表征在单单元和群体水平上逐渐对齐；模型与人类实验数据的比较进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络模型结合适当的学习算法，能够有效模拟联想情绪/效价学习的行为和神经表征特征。

Abstract: Associative emotional learning enables organisms to adaptively link pleasant or unpleasant outcomes to the presence of predictive stimuli. Whereas computational models such as the Rescorla-Wagner model have shed light on this important function, the limitations of these models are also known, especially when they are applied to neural data. The advent of deep neural networks has opened another avenue for modeling associative emotional learning. In this work we proposed a deep neural network model of visual valence processing, consisting of a visual module that encodes complex natural scenes and a module that recognizes their emotional significance in terms of valence, a key dimension of emotion, and tested a novel Pavlovian learning paradigm on the model. The results showed that with learning, the model reproduced several observations from human associative learning studies, including association formation and generalization, and that the neural representations of the conditioned and the unconditioned stimuli became increasingly aligned both at the single unit and at the neural population level. Comparison between the model and human experimental data provided further validation of our approach. This study thus suggests that deep neural network models, when combined with appropriate learning algorithms, can be used to model behavioral and neural signatures of associative emotion/valence learning.

</details>


### [115] [Agents in the Wild: Where Research Meets Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19336)
*Grace Hui Yang,Pranav N. Venkit,Hooman Sedghamiz,Enrico Santus,Victor Dibia,Ioana Baldini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该教程综述了基于大型语言模型的智能体系统从研究原型到生产部署的进展，涵盖推理规划、多智能体协调、评估等核心议题，并结合医药发现和金融系统的案例，分析了成功设计模式及鲁棒性、安全性、可靠性的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体系统在软件工程、科学发现和金融等领域快速从研究原型转向规模化生产部署，部署过程中暴露出的鲁棒性、安全性和可靠性等新挑战亟需系统性的探讨与实践经验总结。

Method: 采用教程形式，汇集研究人员和实践者，通过推理与规划、多智能体协调、评估等专题研讨，结合药物发现和金融系统的应用案例，分析常见设计模式（如验证管线、回退机制、人在环监督）及失效模式的缓解策略。

Result: 提供了该领域的全面概览，以及具体的设计模式、评估清单和模板，用于跨行业的安全可靠部署。

Conclusion: LLM智能体系统的成功部署依赖于对推理、协调和评估的深入理解，以及通过验证管线、回退机制和人在环监督等实践策略来应对生产环境中的失效模式。

Abstract: Agentic systems large language model (LLM) based architectures capable of reasoning, planning, acting, and coordinating with tools and other agents are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to production scale deployments across domains such as software engineering, scientific discovery, and finance. While academic work has emphasized benchmarks and algorithmic innovation, deployment raises new challenges around robustness, safety, and reliability. This tutorial brings together researchers and practitioners to explore advances in reasoning and planning, multi agent coordination, and evaluation, highlighting open challenges arising from deployment experience. Through applied case studies in pharmaceutical discovery and financial systems, we analyze common design patterns that make agentic systems successful, and discuss practical mitigation strategies for failure modes, such as verification pipelines, fallback mechanisms, and human in the loop supervision. Attendees will gain a comprehensive view of the field along with concrete design patterns, evaluation checklists, and templates for safe and reliable deployment across industries.

</details>


### [116] [CodeRescue: Budget-Calibrated Recovery Routing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19338)
*Qijia He,Jiayi Cheng,Chenqian Le,Rui Wang,Xunmei Liu,Yixian Chen,Jie Mei,Zhihao Wang,Xupeng Chen,Yuhuan Chen,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向编程智能体的后失败恢复路由机制，通过监督路由器和保形风险控制层，在预算约束下动态决策是使用廉价模型重试还是升级到昂贵模型，在保持解决率的同时显著降低平均恢复成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本感知系统通常将失败视为级联决策（先用廉价模型，失败后升级到昂贵模型），但在编程场景中，执行反馈使得廉价模型的进一步恢复尝试仍有价值。因此需要回答一个预算约束下的部署问题：智能体何时应该投入更多廉价计算，何时应该升级？

Method: 将失败后的决策形式化为异构动作上的恢复路由问题，从执行轨迹中训练监督路由器。为适应不同预算，引入保形风险控制层，在无需重新训练的情况下选择部署时的成本惩罚，并在可交换性假设下提供边际期望成本控制。

Result: 在五个编程基准的保留失败样本上，廉价恢复和升级展现了互补的成功模式。校准的前沿优于固定动作、仅提示路由器和二元级联基线；在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4设置下，一个CRC校准的前沿点超越了始终升级的解决率，同时仅使用其35%的平均恢复成本。

Conclusion: 监督路由器结合保形风险控制层能有效在预算约束下平衡编程智能体的恢复成本与解决率，实现了成本效率的显著提升。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly operate in executable environments where a failed attempt produces actionable feedback rather than merely an incorrect answer. Existing cost-aware systems typically treat such failures as cascade decisions: try a cheap model first, then escalate hard cases to a stronger and more expensive model. In coding, however, execution feedback can also make further cheap-model recovery worthwhile, raising a budgeted deployment question: when should an agent spend more cheap compute, and when should it escalate? We formulate this post-failure decision as recovery routing over heterogeneous actions and train a supervised router from execution rollouts. To make the same router usable under changing budgets, we add a Conformal Risk Control (CRC) layer that selects a deployment-time cost penalty without retraining and provides marginal expected-cost control under exchangeability. Across held-out failures from five coding benchmarks, cheap recovery and escalation exhibit complementary success patterns. The calibrated frontier improves over fixed actions, prompt-only routers, and a binary cascade baseline; in the main GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4 setting, one CRC-calibrated frontier point exceeds always-escalate solve rate while using 35% of its mean recovery cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Qijia-He/agent-budget-control.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [$\texttt{codesign-mcdp}$: A Python Library for Monotone Co-Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18415)
*Corentin Briat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: codesign-mcdp是一个用于在Censi(2015)框架中表述和求解单调协同设计问题(MCDP)的Python库，实现了反链演算、六种原语设计问题类型、三种组合算子、Kleene不动点求解器以及多种高级构建器，并支持不确定性、在线学习等扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为单调协同设计问题(MCDP)提供一个系统化的Python求解库，使得设计问题可以在功能偏序集与资源偏序集之间进行形式化表述与组合求解。

Method: 该库实现了反链演算作为核心求解机制；提供了六种原语设计问题类型；实现了串联、并联、反馈三种组合算子；使用Kleene不动点求解器处理反馈循环；并提供了声明式MCDPL构建器和模块化System构建器两种高层接口。在此基础上，进一步扩展了集合不确定性、随机不确定性、组合在线学习以及时间域、向量状态和在线协同设计等高级功能。

Result: 构建了一个功能完整的MCDP求解库，支持从基础原语到复杂组合的设计问题求解，并附带一系列工作示例。

Conclusion: codesign-mcdp为单调协同设计问题的形式化表述、组合与求解提供了完备的软件工具，并可通过扩展层支持更丰富的设计场景。

Abstract: $\texttt{codesign-mcdp}$ is a Python library for formulating and solving $\textit{Monotone Co-Design Problems}$ (MCDPs) in the framework of Censi (2015). A design problem is a relation between two posets, a functionality poset $F$ and a resource poset $R$; given a target functionality, the problem asks for the antichain of minimal resources needed to deliver it. Design problems compose under three operators (series, parallel, feedback), and the resulting class is closed under composition. The library implements the antichain calculus, six primitive design-problem types, the three composition operators, a Kleene fixed-point solver, and two high-level builders (an MCDPL-style declarative builder and a modular $\texttt{System}$ builder). Further layers add set-based and stochastic uncertainty, compositional online learning, and temporal, vector-state, and online co-design, alongside a suite of worked examples.

</details>


### [118] [A National-Scale EV Charging Scheduling Framework: Optimal Detour Routing Under Infrastructure Capacity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18453)
*Taner Cokyasar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的优化框架，用于在现有充电基础设施下调度电动汽车长途出行，涵盖不可行性剪枝、动态规划最优调度和容量感知迭代拥塞解决三个阶段。在约270万条轨迹和14,260个充电站的数据上，该框架在128核集群上1.3小时内生成可行调度，并进行了三层经济分析评估EV相对于燃油车的成本竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电动汽车普及率增长，量化现有充电基础设施下长途出行的调度负担和经济成本，对基础设施规划和政策制定至关重要。

Method: 提出三阶段优化框架：(i) 前向可达性启发式不可行性剪枝；(ii) 基于有向无环图的动态规划计算每辆车最优充电调度；(iii) 基于惩罚启发式的容量感知迭代拥塞解决，辅以先到先服务队列回退策略。框架在POLARIS代理交通仿真系统上运行，使用真实充电站数据和全国个人出行需求1%样本。

Result: 在约270万条轨迹、14,260个DC快充站和68,641个充电插头的数据规模下，框架在128核HPC集群上于1.3小时内生成容量可行的调度方案，无需商业优化求解器。同时进行了涵盖运营成本、总拥有成本和分期基础设施投资的三层经济分析。

Conclusion: 该框架可规模化解决电动汽车长途出行充电调度问题，为评估EV相对于燃油车的成本竞争力提供量化工具，对充电基础设施规划和政策具有参考价值。

Abstract: As electric vehicle (EV) adoption grows, quantifying the scheduling burden and economic cost of long-distance travel under the existing charging infrastructure becomes increasingly important for infrastructure planning and policy. This paper presents a scalable, optimization-based framework for scheduling EV charging stops along real-world charging stations and simulated long-distance personal vehicle trajectories across the United States using POLARIS. Taking the existing charging network as fixed input, the framework minimizes total detour and queuing costs for each vehicle while respecting plug capacity constraints at each station. The methodology proceeds in three phases: (i) infeasibility pruning via a forward-pass reachability heuristic, (ii) per-vehicle optimal charging schedule computation via dynamic programming on a directed acyclic graph, and (iii) capacity-aware iterative congestion resolution through a penalty-based heuristic that augments detour costs at congested stations, with a first-in, first-out queue fallback. Applied to approximately 2.7M origin--destination vehicle trajectories derived from a 1\% sample of national personal travel demand within the POLARIS agent-based transportation simulation framework and covering 14,260 DC fast charging stations with 68,641 plugs from the Alternative Fuels Station Locator, the framework produces capacity-feasible schedules in under 1.3 hours on a 128-core high-performance computing cluster without requiring any commercial optimization solver. A three-tier economic analysis spanning operational costs, total cost of ownership, and amortized infrastructure investment is conducted to evaluate EV cost competitiveness relative to internal combustion engine vehicles across scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [Revisit escape path for infinite unit strip forest and unit broadworm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18563)
*Zhipeng Deng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在前期工作的基础上，提出了Zalgerving无限单位带状森林与单位阔蠕虫逃逸路径问题的新方法，将问题转化为区间覆盖与约束泛函最小化问题，并通过凸优化求解，最后证明闭合逃逸路径的最优解为单位常宽曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决贝尔曼"迷失森林"问题的特例（无限单位带状森林与单位阔蠕虫），提供解析公式并推广分析，克服此前仅依赖几何方法的局限性。

Method: 将逃逸路径问题重新表述为区间覆盖问题，进一步转化为约束泛函最小化问题，通过直接离散化将其转化为凸优化问题求解。

Result: 提出了新的分析和解析公式；将问题转化为凸优化可解形式；在闭合逃逸路径情形下，证明了最优解为单位常宽曲线。

Conclusion: 所提方法可泛化至各类线段分析，且闭合路径的最优解具有单位常宽几何特性。

Abstract: Building on our previous general computational solution to Bellman's Lost-in-a-Forest Problem, we present a new approach and analytical formulas for the previously well-known escape path for the infinite unit-strip forest and unit broadworm by Zalgaller. Earlier studies addressed these problems exclusively through geometric methods. We reformulated the problem as an interval-cover problem and then formulated it as a constrained functional minimization problem. This constrained functional minimization can be directly discretized and subsequently solved as a convex optimization. Furthermore, we extend the analysis of various line segment. Finally, we show that, in the case of a closed escape path for the unit strip, the optimal solution is a curve of constant unit width.

</details>


### [120] [Fragility of Minimum-Variance Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18624)
*Daniel Ovalle,Carl D. Laird,Ignacio E. Grossmann,Javier Peña*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入块对角相关结构导出最小方差组合的解析解，揭示了阈值效应导致的组合脆弱性，并提出了基于结构化收缩的稳健化方法，在异质波动率下通过稀疏化避免高风险资产暴露，实证表明该方法在降低样本外方差和换手率方面优于传统基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小方差组合对协方差估计误差高度敏感，现有全局收缩方法缺乏解析解释且调参复杂，本文旨在从解析角度揭示脆弱性根源并设计理论指导下的稳健化方案。

Method: 对长期限仓最小方差组合施加块对角相关结构以导出解析解；基于阈值效应分析提出结构化收缩方案（相关性感知与相关性忽略两种变体），通过聚类近似扩展到一般协方差矩阵。

Result: 同质波动率下相关性忽略收缩最优；异质波动率下相关性感知收缩通过诱导稀疏性避免高风险暴露表现最佳；所提方法在样本外方差和换手率上持续优于经典和聚类基准。

Conclusion: 结构化收缩方法具有解析基础、微调需求低且保持与最小方差解一致，为稳健投资组合构建提供了原则性且实用的框架。

Abstract: Minimum-variance portfolios are well known to be highly sensitive to covariance estimation error. In this paper, we show that by imposing a block diagonal correlation structure, we can derive closed-form expressions for long-only minimum-variance portfolios that make this fragility explicit. These analytical solutions reveal that fragility is driven by threshold effects arising from the interaction between correlation structure and the assets' volatilities. Motivated by the latter insight, we propose robustification approaches that can be interpreted as structured shrinkage schemes that selectively attenuate unstable coupling while preserving the dominant risk structure. Unlike global shrinkage techniques, the proposed corrections are analytically grounded, require minimal tuning, and remain closely aligned with the minimum-variance solution. The framework extends naturally to general covariance matrices through clustering-based approximations. Empirical results on controlled simulations highlight a clear regime dependence. In homogeneous volatility settings, correlation-oblivious shrinkage achieves the best trade-off between risk and stability. In contrast, under heterogeneous volatility, correlation-aware shrinkage performs best by inducing sparsity and avoiding exposure to high-risk assets. Across regimes, the proposed methods consistently reduce out-of-sample variance and turnover relative to classical and clustering-based benchmarks, providing a principled and practical approach to robust portfolio construction.

</details>


### [121] [Directional Derivatives and Error Bounds of Merit Functions in Vector Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18781)
*Yu Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了向量优化问题中价值函数θ的方向导数与误差界，建立了方向导数、解集几何与误差界之间的完整理论桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为向量优化算法收敛性分析提供基础理论工具，填补价值函数方向导数与解集几何之间联系的空白。

Method: 利用定向距离函数Δ_C和弱*紧凸集K的双表示，推导方向导数显式公式；通过13种全局误差界条件的等价性刻画，结合单位球面最小方向导数的均匀负性条件建立全局误差界特征。

Result: (1) 证明了θ的凹性与Lipschitz连续性及对偶表示；(2) 在弱有效解处获得方向导数显式公式并刻画零方向导数锥；(3) 建立了13种全局误差界条件的等价性；(4) 全局误差界由单位球面上最小方向导数的均匀负性条件刻画；(5) 确定了最优局部误差界常数为1/φ(̄x)（当φ(̄x)>0时）。

Conclusion: 建立了价值函数方向导数与向量优化解集几何之间的完整理论框架，为向量优化算法（如梯度型方法）的收敛性分析奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of directional derivatives and error bounds for the merit function $θ(x)=\sup_{a\in A}\bigl(-Δ_C(F(x)-F(a))\bigr)$ associated with the vector optimization problem $\operatorname{Min}_C\{F(x):x\in A\}$, where $Δ_C$ is the oriented distance function. We first prove that $θ$ is concave and Lipschitz continuous on the whole space and derive its dual representation via the weak$^*$ compact convex set $K=\overline{\operatorname{co}}^{w^*}(S(C^+))$. At a weakly efficient solution $\bar x$, we obtain the explicit formula $θ'(\bar x;d)=\min_{y^*\in W(\bar x)}\langle y^*,F(d)\rangle$ with $W(\bar{x})=\{y^*\in K:F^*y^*\in -N_A(\bar{x})\}$, characterize the zero-directional-derivative cone, and prove that, under a local error bound condition, the tangent cone to the solution set is $T_{E_w}(\bar x)=T_A(\bar x)\cap T_{\widehat A}(\bar x)=\{d\in T_A(\bar x):θ'(\bar x;d)=0\}$. We establish the equivalence of thirteen distinct global error bound conditions, including characterizations via linear regularity, the global slope, an asymptotic condition, and perturbation stability. A central result shows that the global error bound property for $θ$ on the feasible set $A$ is characterized by a uniform negativity condition on the unit-sphere minimal directional derivative, namely $\sup_{x \in A \setminus E_w} \varphi(x) < 0$. We also determine the optimal local error bound constant precisely as $1/\varphi(\bar x)$ when $\varphi(\bar x)>0$, and provide a counterexample demonstrating that an additional directional condition is essential when $\varphi(\bar x)=0$. These results provide a complete bridge between the directional derivative of the merit function and the geometry of the solution set, offering fundamental tools for the convergence analysis of algorithms in vector optimization.

</details>


### [122] [A frugal primal-dual splitting with minimal lifting over arbitrary rooted trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18932)
*Feng Xue,Hui Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于树状图结构的节俭原始-对偶分裂方法，通过层次化节点和边设计，实现了最小提升的单调包含问题求解，支持灵活的双变量和余强制性元素分配，可推广至多种分布式计算场景，并证明了O(1/k)的遍历收敛率和o(1/k)的渐近正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原始-对偶分裂方法在处理涉及余强制性算子、线性组合和平行和的复杂结构单调包含问题时，存在算法灵活性不足、分布式计算适应性差以及收敛速度理论不完善等问题。本文旨在开发一种既节俭（最小提升）又灵活可扩展的算法框架。

Method: 通过定义树状结构图中的层次化节点和边，将双变量和余强制性元素任意分配到原始节点上，构建了一种节俭的原始-对偶分裂方法。该方法涵盖Douglas-Rachford分裂在各类图上的推广以及Chambolle-Pock算法的并行化版本，并引入乘积Hilbert空间中的重构技术进行收敛性分析。

Result: 所提方法在结构化凸最小化问题上达到O(1/k)的原始-对偶间隙遍历收敛率，并进一步推导出渐近正则性的o(1/k)收敛速度。算法自然恢复并扩展了Douglas-Rachford分裂和并行Chambolle-Pock方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的节俭原始-对偶分裂框架在最小提升条件下实现了高度灵活的结构化单调包含求解，支持层次化同步分布式计算（集中式或去中心化），并提供了完整的收敛率理论保证。

Abstract: We develop a frugal primal-dual splitting with minimal lifting for solving structured monotone inclusions, involving cocoercive operators, linear compositions and parallel sums. This is established by defining hierarchical nodes and edges between them over a tree-structured graph, with arbitrary assignments of dual variables and cocoercive elements to primal nodes. This arbitrariness allows a great flexibility in terms of level-synchronous distributed computing, such as centralized or decentralized. The particular instances naturally extend the Douglas--Rachford splitting on various graphs, recover the parallel Chambolle--Pock, and solve a class of structured convex minimization problems with O(1/k) ergodic rate for its primal-dual gap function. Furthermore, we introduce a reformulation technique in a product Hilbert space to facilitate the convergence analysis, specifically to derive the o(1/k)-rate of asymptotic regularity.

</details>


### [123] [Evaluating DV/CV-QKD Architectures for SAFE Long-Term Secure Storage: A Risk Model and ILP-Based Cost Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19010)
*Alireza Tasdighi,Romain Alléaume*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一成本建模与优化框架，用于评估混合DV/CV-QKD基础设施在SAFE长期安全存储协议下的性能。该框架结合信息论与计算维度的密码耐久性，通过随机整数线性规划与样本平均近似管道，在传输预算与安全约束下确定成本最优的SAFE拓扑。在巴黎等实际城域网络中的评估表明：最优架构高度依赖场景，混合与同构模态均可自发涌现；更重要的是，最小QKD链路容量与基础设施成本呈非单调关系——提高每链路密钥率可能通过转向更经济的CV方案降低总成本，但进一步增加容量不一定带来额外节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QKD网络设计多基于物理层理想化模型，缺乏对多十年时间跨度的信息论安全耐久性、实际工业实现约束以及混合DV/CV模态共存场景下的成本-安全联合优化方法。

Method: 耦合定量风险模型与随机整数线性规划(ILP)公式，结合样本平均近似(SAA)管道，纳入下一代工业实现与多路复用共存规范，在传输预算和安全约束下优化SAFE拓扑结构。

Result: 在巴黎城域网络等真实基础设施上的数值实验表明：最优架构高度场景依赖，混合与纯DV/CV模态皆可自然产生；最小每链路密钥率与总成本呈非单调关系——适度提高容量可促使向经济CV方案迁移从而降低成本，但过高的容量要求并不带来额外节约。

Conclusion: 混合DV/CV-QKD部署的经济最优性取决于具体场景，且链路容量与成本的非单调关系对下一代QKD基础设施的规划具有重要指导意义，提示不应简单追求高密钥率而需考虑整体经济性。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified cost-modeling and optimization framework designed to evaluate hybrid discrete-variable/continuous-variable quantum key distribution (DV/CV-QKD) infrastructures operating within the SAFE (Secure and Efficient) long-term storage (LTSS) protocol. Our methodology jointly addresses the information-theoretic and computational dimensions of cryptographic durability over multi-decade horizons by coupling a quantitative risk model with a stochastic integer linear programming (ILP) formulation. Going beyond idealized physics-informed limits, the framework incorporates realistic next-generation industrial implementations and multiplexed coexistence specifications, together with a sample-average approximation (SAA) pipeline, to determine cost-optimal and structurally feasible SAFE topologies under transmission-budget and security constraints. The proposed framework is evaluated on both randomized synthetic deployments and realistic metropolitan-scale QKD infrastructures, including the Paris Metro-Scale and Greater Paris networks, to characterize the interplay between network topology, QKD modality, and deployment cost while enforcing a target global compromise tolerance $ε$. Numerical results show that the economically optimal architecture is highly scenario-dependent and that both hybrid and homogeneous modality allocations naturally emerge from the optimization. More importantly, the analysis reveals a non-monotonic relationship between the minimum required QKD link capacity and the infrastructure cost: contrary to intuition, increasing the minimum required per-link secret-key rate may reduce the overall deployment cost by enabling a global transition toward more economical CV-based solutions, whereas further capacity increases do not necessarily yield additional savings.

</details>


### [124] [Graphical stability of set-valued integrals under measure perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19035)
*Tam Le*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非光滑优化中参数化集值积分在概率测度弱扰动下的稳定性，证明了在紧参数集上、联合外部下半连续且满足超线性可积条件的集值被积函数，其集值积分映射在弱收敛概率测度序列下以图形方式收敛于过剩距离，并讨论了假设的尖锐性及在随机广义方程、马尔可夫采样非光滑优化等场景的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为非光滑优化中出现的采样和近似方案提供理论支撑，研究参数化集值积分在概率分布弱扰动下的稳定性，建立集值期望的测度近似的统一稳定性原理。

Method: 证明在紧参数集上，联合外部下半连续且满足超线性可积条件的凸紧集值被积函数，其集值积分映射沿任意弱收敛的概率测度序列在过剩距离下图形收敛。

Result: 建立了集值积分映射在弱测度收敛下的图形收敛性，证明了超线性包络条件的紧致性以及联合外部下半连续的必要性，并获得了随机广义方程解集的外部稳定性。

Conclusion: 该稳定性原理统一处理了集值期望的测度逼近问题，在马尔可夫采样非光滑优化、磨光算子平滑、参数依赖分布动力学等场景中具有应用价值。

Abstract: Motivated by sampling and approximation schemes arising in nonsmooth optimization, we study the stability of parameterized set-valued integrals under weak perturbations of the underlying probability distribution. For a compact parameter set, we show that compact convex-valued and jointly outer semicontinuous integrands induce set-valued integral maps that converge graphically in excess distance along any weakly convergent sequence of probability measures. The result holds under a superlinear integrability condition and provides a unified stability principle for measure approximations of set-valued expectations. We discuss the sharpness of the assumptions through examples. In particular, we emphasize that joint outer semicontinuity is required in general and that the superlinear envelope condition is tight relative to the classical i.i.d. empirical setting. As a consequence, we obtain outer stability of solution sets for stochastic generalized equations. We illustrate the stability result in several settings, including stochastic nonsmooth optimization with Markovian sampling, smoothing by mollifiers, and parameter-dependent distributional dynamics.

</details>


### [125] [Industrial Application of a Multi-Disciplinary Design Optimization with Uncertainties to a Pair of Telecommunication Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19045)
*Olivier Sapin,Loïc Cousin,Nicolas Roussouly,Anne Gazaix,François Gallard,Matthias De Lozzo,Xavier Fosse,Nicolas Sarda,Gaspard Berthelin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在卫星设计中采用不确定性下的多学科设计优化方法，通过灵敏度分析和基于可靠性的设计优化，相比传统安全裕度方法，在保证约束可行性概率的前提下，将性能损失降低了66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卫星设计中添加安全裕度的方法虽然能应对不确定性，但往往以牺牲性能为代价。本文旨在通过将不确定性纳入设计问题定义，实现更优的鲁棒性与性能平衡。

Method: 采用不确定性下的多学科设计优化方法，结合不确定性量化技术（灵敏度分析和基于可靠性的设计优化），应用于一对以堆叠配置发射的通信卫星工业用例。

Result: 相比添加预定义安全裕度的传统方法，该方法在保证约束高概率可行性的前提下，将性能损失降低了66%。

Conclusion: 将不确定性纳入卫星设计问题中，能够在保证约束可行性的同时显著降低性能损失，比传统安全裕度方法更有效。

Abstract: In satellite design, it is common practice to add safety margins to the constraints to achieve conservative solutions that are robust to uncertainties. This robustness often comes at the expense of performance and it may be more appropriate to include uncertainties in the definition of the design problem. This work addresses such a challenge by applying techniques of multidisciplinary design optimization under uncertainty to an industrial use case. The latter is a pair of telecommunication satellites launched together in a stacked configuration. Each satellite is a strongly coupled multidisciplinary system while the two satellites are not coupled at all. This use case can be extended to an arbitrary number of satellites. By considering uncertainty quantification techniques such as sensitivity analysis and reliability-based design optimization, this study demonstrates that accounting for uncertainties in the design problem results in a 66% reduction in performance loss compared to the adding of a predefined safety margins, while guaranteeing the feasibility of constraints with high probability.

</details>


### [126] [Decentralized Linearized Consensus ADMM with Efficient Quantized Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19074)
*Boyu Han,Xu Du,Karl H. Johansson,Apostolos I. Rikos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合不精确一致ADMM与有限时间去中心化量化通信的分布式优化算法，适用于有向图，仅需量化信息且无需精确求解子问题，实现了全局线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化优化相比集中式方法在大规模问题中具有可扩展性和鲁棒性优势，但现有方法通常假设精确通信或精确子问题求解，且多数限于无向图。本文旨在克服这些限制。

Method: 将不精确一致ADMM (IC-ADMM) 与有限时间去中心化量化通信算法相结合，使得算法：(i) 适用于有向通信图，(ii) 仅需量化局部信息而非精确值，(iii) 不要求精确求解局部子问题。

Result: 在局部目标函数强凸且L-光滑的假设下，算法保证全局线性收敛到最优解的邻域内；数值实验表明在收敛速度和通信效率上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法在放宽精确通信、精确子问题求解及无向图限制的同时，仍能保持线性收敛性能，是一种兼具实用性和理论保证的去中心化优化方法。

Abstract: Distributed optimization offers significant advantages over centralized methods in terms of scalability and robustness when solving large-scale problems. In this paper, we propose a novel decentralized optimization algorithm that integrates Inexact Consensus ADMM (IC-ADMM) with a finite-time decentralized quantized communication algorithm. The proposed method enjoys three main benefits: (i) it operates on directed communication graphs, (ii) it requires only quantized local information instead of exact values, and (iii) it does not rely on solving the local subproblems exactly. Under the assumption that each node's local objective is strongly convex and L-smooth, the algorithm is guaranteed to achieve global linear convergence to a neighborhood of the optimal solution. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate its advantages over existing methods in terms of convergence speed and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [127] [From Operations to Elderly Care Outcomes: A Thematic Review of Industrial Engineering and Decision-Support Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19075)
*Shayan Farhang Pazhooh,Fereshteh Parvaresh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对30篇运筹学与老年护理交叉领域的 seminal 研究进行了主题综述，将文献分为家庭医疗运营、多药管理和临床时间治疗三类。分析揭示了从静态确定性模型向集成人工智能的动态随机框架的方法论演进，但指出当前研究存在关键转化鸿沟：过度聚焦于流程层优化（如人员排班），难以将运营效率转化为可测量的临床结果，且缺乏连接医院与社区护理的整体模型。研究提出了一个从孤立任务转向集成化多层级决策的概念框架，强调通过数字孪生和大语言模型等新兴技术实现鲁棒、智能的老年护理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球人口老龄化对医疗系统造成严峻挑战，需要高效、公平且以患者为中心的护理模式。尽管运筹学提供了强大的优化和决策支持工具，但当前应用仍然碎片化，缺乏系统性的整合视角。

Method: 采用主题综述方法，选取30篇运筹学与老年护理交叉领域的代表性研究，将其分类为家庭医疗运营、多药管理和临床时间治疗三大主题，分析其方法论演化趋势和研究缺口。

Result: 发现了从静态确定性模型向集成AI的动态随机框架的方法论演化趋势；识别出关键转化鸿沟：现有研究过度聚焦流程层优化（如人员排班），而非临床结果；发现医院-社区护理衔接模型严重缺乏。

Conclusion: 提出了一个从孤立运营任务转向集成化、多层级决策的概念框架，强调需要鲁棒系统分析、人本设计和通过数字孪生、大语言模型等新兴技术推动护理智能化，以弥合理论运营指标与患者健康结果之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The rapid growth of the global aging population presents severe challenges to healthcare systems, necessitating efficient, equitable, and patient-centered care models. While Industrial Engineering and Operations Research (OR) provide robust optimization and decision-support tools to address these multidimensional complexities, current applications often remain fragmented. This paper presents a thematic review of 30 seminal studies at the intersection of OR and elderly care, categorizing the literature into home healthcare operations, polypharmacy management, and clinical chronotherapy. Our analysis highlights a significant methodological evolution from static, deterministic models toward dynamic and stochastic frameworks integrated with artificial intelligence (AI). Despite these advancements, a critical translational gap persists: the current OR literature is heavily dominated by process-level optimizations, such as staff routing, and struggles to translate these operational efficiencies into measurable clinical outcomes. Furthermore, holistic models bridging the transition between hospital and community care remain critically underexplored. To develop resilient and smart healthcare systems, this study proposes a conceptual framework that shifts the research focus from isolated operational tasks to integrated, multi-level decision-making. We emphasize the critical need for robust systems analysis, human-inclusive design, and the smartification of care through emerging digital technologies - including digital twins and large language models - to successfully bridge the gap between theoretical operational metrics and tangible patient-level health outcomes.

</details>


### [128] [Active Disturbance Rejection for Boundary Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19144)
*Jukka-Pekka Humaloja,Lassi Paunonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对抽象边界控制系统和受控偏微分方程，考虑存在未知输入扰动和未建模输入非线性的情况，利用自抗扰控制方法设计控制器，实现对输入扰动的抑制，并确保闭环系统在具有共位输入和输出的一类边界控制系统中的稳定性和外部适定性。主要结果被应用于一维波动方程和热方程的控制器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决边界控制系统中存在的一般未知输入扰动和未建模输入非线性所带来的控制难题，提升系统的抗干扰能力和稳定性。

Method: 采用自抗扰控制（ADRC）方法，针对具有共位输入和输出的边界控制系统设计控制器。

Result: 所设计的控制器能够有效抑制输入扰动，并实现闭环系统的稳定性和外部适定性。

Conclusion: ADRC方法适用于一类具有共位输入输出的边界控制系统，能够处理未知输入扰动和非线性，并成功应用于波动方程和热方程的控制器设计。

Abstract: We consider stabilisation of abstract boundary control systems and controlled partial differential equations with general unknown input disturbances and unmodeled nonlinearities at the input. We utilise the active disturbance rejection control approach to design a controller which rejects the input disturbance and achieves stability and external well-posedness of the closed-loop system for a class of boundary control systems with collocated inputs and outputs. We apply our main results to design controllers for one-dimensional wave and heat equations.

</details>


### [129] [A relaxed-inertial proximal point algorithm for strongly quasiconvex equilibrium problems on Hadamard manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19164)
*Luisa Marie Després,Nicholas Pischke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Hadamard流形上由伪单调且强拟凸双函数生成的均衡问题的邻近点型方法，结合惯性步与过松弛技术，首次将此类方法从欧氏空间推广到非线性设置，并提供了显式、快速且均匀的收敛速率，同时弱化或去除了部分先前假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将欧氏空间中针对强拟凸优化的惯性-过松弛邻近点方法推广到Hadamard流形（非正截面曲率的完备单连通黎曼流形）上的均衡问题，这是首次在流形设置下处理过松弛项。

Method: 采用邻近点类型方法，在标准邻近步之外引入惯性步和后续过松弛步骤（在Hadamard流形语境下首次处理）；利用作者先前工作中针对强拟凸优化的定量分析方法来提供收敛性论证。

Result: 给出了迭代点列到解的显式、快速且非常均匀的收敛速率；将Grad、Lara和Marcavillaca在有限维欧氏空间的结果首次推广到非线性设置，且即使在欧氏情形下这些定量估计也是新的；能够弱化甚至完全去除部分先前假设。

Conclusion: 该有效方法提供了对相关假设的细粒度视角，允许在更宽松的条件下保证收敛，拓展了均衡问题求解方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We study a proximal point type method for approximating solutions to equilibrium problems generated by pseudomonotone and strongly quasiconvex bifunctions over Hadamard manifolds, that is complete simply connected Riemannian manifolds of nonpositive sectional curvature. Next to the usual proximal step, the method we consider also incorporates an inertia step together with a subsequent over-relaxation, the latter of which is treated in the context of Hadamard manifolds, to our knowledge, for the first time. Making use of a quantitative approach towards such proximal methods for strongly quasiconvex optimization developed by the authors in previous work, we in particular provide effective arguments for the convergence of the method, yielding explicit, fast and very uniform rates of convergence for the distance of the iterates towards the solution. These results extend previous work by Grad, Lara and Marcavillaca on such a method over finite-dimensional Euclidean spaces for the first time to a nonlinear setting, with the quantitative estimates already being novel in the Euclidean case. In particular, our effective approach allows for a fine-grained view on the assumptions on the surrounding objects, so that we are able to either weaken or even fully discharge some previous assumptions.

</details>


### [130] [A Complete Characterization of Optimal Subgradient Methods for Lipschitz Convex Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19240)
*Aaron Zoll,Benjamin Grimmer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对M-利普希茨凸优化中固定步长一阶方法的最优性进行了完整刻画，证明了所有最优方法均可通过构造性方法导出，并给出了最优方法集合的多面体表示，最后证明了不存在任何时间最优的固定步长次梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已知初始距离界D和Lipschitz常数M的凸优化问题中，已有研究发现了多种参数化的最优固定步长方法，但缺乏对这些最优方法的完整刻画与统一理解。

Method: 通过证明乘子（proof multipliers）这一工具，对所有最优固定步长一阶方法给出了多面体表示，并建立了与构造性方法（constructive approach）的联系。

Result: (1) 完整刻画了所有最优固定步长方法；(2) 所有最优方法均可从构造性方法导出；(3) 最优方法集合具有多面体结构；(4) 不存在任意时间最优（anytime optimal）的固定步长次梯度方法。

Conclusion: 固定步长一阶方法的最优性已被完整刻画，但其"任意时间最优"性质无法实现，这对实际算法设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: We consider the design of optimal fixed-step first-order methods for $M$-Lipschitz convex optimization given $\|x_0-x_\star\|\leq D$. Prior works have identified several distinct fixed-step methods, parameterized by a matrix of stepsizes $W$, with the (information-theoretic) minimax optimal rate $MD/\sqrt{N+1}$ of objective gap convergence. We provide a complete characterization of every optimal fixed-step method. Moreover, we show every optimal fixed-step method can be derived from the constructive approach of~\cite{constructive_approach} and provide a polyhedral representation of the set of optimal methods through proof multipliers. From this characterization, we show that no anytime optimal fixed-step subgradient methods exist.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [FALCON-Discover: Discovering Concentrated False-Confidence Regions for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18278)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FALCON-Discover框架，通过置信度、局部支持、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性等差异信号来发现模型预测中"高置信度但错误"的局部区域（即假置信集中现象）。实验表明，基于差异信号的排序方法在发现危险错误方面显著优于传统校准方法，且最佳检测器因数据集而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的校准评估通常是聚合层面的，忽略了局部区域中"高置信度但错误"这一最危险失败模式。作者旨在系统性地研究这种假置信集中现象，并将其从单一校准分数问题重新定义为区域发现问题。

Method: 提出FALCON-Discover框架，一种事后、模型无关的检测方法。利用置信度、局部支持、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性四种差异信号对预测进行排序。在7个二分类表格数据集上，使用4种随机种子、5折交叉拟合，结合XGBoost和CatBoost等强学习器进行实验。

Result: 假置信集中在不同数据集中普遍存在但具有依赖特定数据分布的特征。在主要置信阈值上，基于差异信号的排序方法在最强数据分布下显著优于基于校准或信任评分的最强基线，而原始置信度几乎无法捕捉危险错误。最佳检测器因数据集而异：需要组合多种线索时，学习的差异信号最强；局部决策脆弱性主导时，基于稳定性的排序效果最佳。

Conclusion: 危险的过度自信应被视为一个"家族级"的发现问题，而非单一分数的校准问题。未来的校准策略应明确针对置信度、支持度和稳定性分化的区域进行优化。

Abstract: Calibration is usually evaluated in aggregate, but the most dangerous failures are often local: predictions that remain highly confident despite being wrong. We study this failure mode as false-confidence concentration, the extent to which confident errors occupy compact, discoverable regions of prediction space. We introduce FALCON-Discover, a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework that ranks predictions using discrepancy signals from confidence, local support, neighborhood agreement, and perturbation stability. Across seven binary tabular datasets, four seeds, five-fold cross-fitting, and strong learners including XGBoost and CatBoost, we find that false-confidence concentration is recurrent but regime-dependent. At the main confidence threshold, discrepancy-based ranking substantially outperforms the strongest validation-selected calibration or trust-scoring baseline in the strongest regimes, while raw confidence recovers little dangerous-error mass. The best detector varies across datasets: learned discrepancy is strongest when multiple cues must be combined, whereas stability-centered ranking works best when local decisional fragility dominates. These results show that dangerous overconfidence is better treated as a family-level discovery problem than as a single-score calibration problem, and motivate calibration strategies that explicitly target regions where confidence, support, and stability diverge.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond Output-Space Calibration: Spectral Evidence Bundling for Selective Reliability Estimation in Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18279)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于验证门控的固定标签可靠性策略，通过结合输出置信度与频谱特征（频带能量、熵、峰值优势、周期支持、相位稳定性）来估计时序分类的可靠性，在8个数据集和8种骨干网络上将Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.786，FalseConf@0.9降至0.094。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序分类的后验校准仅重映射输出分数，但忽略了三个可靠性缺口：相同置信度可能对应不同的时间序列支撑、平均校准可能掩盖高置信度错误、输出空间重校准缺乏输入层面的可审计性。

Method: 提出验证门控的固定标签可靠性策略：保持骨干网络预测不变，结合输出侧线索与全样本频谱描述符（频带能量、熵、峰值优势、周期支持、相位稳定性）形成标量可靠性估计及诊断性频带证据。验证门控仅在正确性排名提升且不违反FalseConf@0.9或AURC容差时才启用频谱条件化，否则回退到输出空间基线。

Result: 在8个UCR/UEA数据集、8种时序骨干网络和标准校准器上，无约束方法将Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.779；验证门控策略进一步将Corr-AURC提升至0.786，FalseConf@0.9降至0.094。

Conclusion: 时序分类器的可靠性估计受益于将输出置信度与频谱证据相结合，而验证门控机制可防止无支持的频谱条件化带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration for time-series classification usually remaps output scores, but deployment decisions such as trust, abstention, and review depend on whether a confident prediction is supported by the current temporal signal. We address three time-series reliability gaps: identical confidence values can hide different temporal support, average calibration can miss false high-confidence errors, and output-space recalibration offers limited input-linked auditability. We introduce a validation-gated fixed-label reliability policy that keeps the backbone prediction unchanged while estimating whether it should be trusted. The method combines output-side cues with whole-sample spectral descriptors, including band energy, entropy, peak dominance, period support, and phase stability, to form a scalar reliability estimate and diagnostic band-level evidence. A validation gate enables spectral conditioning only when correctness ranking improves without breaching FalseConf@0.9 or AURC tolerances; otherwise it reverts to the safer output-space baseline. Across eight heterogeneous UCR/UEA datasets, eight time-series backbone families, and standard recalibrators, the unconstrained method improves fixed-label selective-reliability metrics on the matched evaluation subset, raising Corr-AURC from 0.693 to 0.779. The validation-gated policy further improves Corr-AURC to 0.786 and reduces FalseConf@0.9 to 0.094. These results suggest that reliability estimation for time-series classifiers benefits from bundling output confidence with spectral evidence, while validation gating prevents unsupported spectral conditioning.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Single-Dimensional Compression: The Compound Sparsity Frontier of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18280)
*Chao Han,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将静态参数剪枝（低秩近似+通道剪枝）与动态逐token层跳过相结合的复合稀疏框架，在同等总稀疏度下比单一压缩机制更有效地延缓性能退化，并发现跨维度干扰与近平衡分配策略最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM压缩方法（静态参数剪枝或动态token级计算）在稀疏度过高时性能急剧下降，本文探索将两种机制结合是否能通过分散压缩负担来延缓这种退化。

Method: 先通过低秩近似和通道剪枝获得静态压缩骨干网络，再引入轻量级路由器实现逐token动态层跳过，独立控制参数稀疏度和token级计算稀疏度。

Result: 在语言理解和建模基准上，复合稀疏在同等总稀疏度下持续优于单一机制，延缓理解任务性能衰减点，保持更强的建模性能。参数剪枝与token跳过的跨维度干扰存在，近平衡分配最有效。

Conclusion: 复合压缩是提升LLM压缩效果的实用方法，但存在更广泛的跨维度稀疏边界限制了进一步压缩。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compressed through static parameter pruning or dynamic token-level computation, yet aggressive sparsification can trigger rapid performance degradation beyond an essential sparsity boundary. This work asks \emph{whether combining these two mechanisms can delay such degradation by distributing the compression burden}. We study a minimalist compound sparsity framework that first applies low-rank approximation and channel pruning to obtain a statically compressed backbone, and then introduces lightweight routers for per-token dynamic layer skipping. This design enables independent control of parameter sparsity and token-level computation sparsity. Experiments across language understanding and modeling benchmarks show that compound sparsity consistently outperforms single-mechanism compression under the same total sparsity, delaying the decay point on understanding tasks and preserving stronger modeling performance. Further analysis reveals cross-dimensional interference between parameter pruning and token skipping, and shows that near-balanced allocation is most effective under a fixed sparsity budget. These results demonstrate that compound compression provides a practical way to improve LLM compression, while revealing a broader cross-dimensional sparsity boundary that ultimately limits further compression. Code will be available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning.

</details>


### [134] [ALAS: Additive Learnable Alpha-Stable Kernels for Flexible Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18282)
*Weibo Huang,Cheng Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ALAS核函数族，通过对称α稳定谱成分构建高斯过程核，能够自适应学习数据的平滑度并捕捉光滑趋势与尖锐不规则性。实验表明其在多种基准测试和真实代理任务中表现强劲且鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化的成功高度依赖于所选核函数是否匹配目标函数的未知结构，而传统核函数往往无法灵活适应不同平滑度的目标函数。

Method: 基于对称α稳定谱成分构建灵活的高斯过程核函数族ALAS；通过学习稳定性参数α自适应调整有效平滑度；提出两种参数化形式：ALAS（单成分联合谱调制）和ALAS-Sep（可分离变体，学习维度级尾部分布以提升可分解目标函数的鲁棒性）。

Result: 在标准基准测试和真实代理任务上，ALAS在多种设置下均表现出强劲且鲁棒的优化性能。

Conclusion: ALAS作为一种自适应的核函数族，能够有效匹配不同结构的目标函数，提升了贝叶斯优化的鲁棒性和适用性。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization is widely used for expensive black-box optimization, yet its success often depends on choosing a kernel that matches the objective's unknown structure. In this work, we propose ALAS, a flexible Gaussian Process kernel family built from symmetric $α$-stable spectral components. By learning the stability parameter $α$, ALAS adapts its effective smoothness from data, capturing both smooth trends and sharp irregularities. We present two parameterizations: ALAS, a single stationary component with joint spectral modulation, and ALAS-Sep, a separable variant that learns dimension-wise tail behavior to improve robustness on approximately decomposable objectives. Experiments on standard benchmarks and real-world surrogates demonstrate strong and robust performance across diverse settings.

</details>


### [135] [FedCC: A Low-Resource Federated Adaptation of Foundation Models for Robust Corpus Callosum localization in Fetal Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18283)
*Alessandro Di Matteo,Sara Moccia,Giuseppe Rizzo,Gianpaolo Grisolia,Ricciarda Raffaelli,Lorenzo Vasciaveo,Francesco D'Antonio,Maria Chiara Fiorentino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCC，一种基于联邦学习的胎儿超声图像胼胝体定位框架。该方法将冻结的DINOv2骨干网络与轻量级YOLO检测头结合，并引入低秩适应（LoRA）模块实现参数高效微调。在多中心数据集（10,970帧超声图像，58名孕妇，三个临床中心）上，FedCC在FedAvg策略下达到平均mAP@50为0.857、F1-score为0.803，可训练参数从24.4M降至2.9M（约8.5倍通信成本降低），实现了隐私保护与资源高效的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿超声图像中胼胝体（CC）的准确定位对早期识别神经发育异常至关重要。然而，超声成像存在低对比度、散斑噪声以及胼胝体解剖结构变异大等固有限制，使得定位任务极具挑战性。现有方法难以兼顾多中心临床场景下的数据隐私、通信效率和计算资源限制。

Method: 提出FedCC框架：1）采用冻结的DINOv2作为视觉骨干网络提取通用特征；2）结合轻量级YOLO检测头进行目标定位；3）引入低秩适应（LoRA）模块，仅优化和交换少量参数；4）基于FedAvg聚合策略实现联邦学习，无需数据共享。

Result: 在多中心数据集（10,970帧，58名孕妇，三个临床中心）上，FedCC（DINOv2+LoRA+FedAvg）达到平均mAP@50为0.857、F1-score为0.803，优于全微调和编码器冻结基线。可训练参数从24.4M降至2.9M，通信成本降低约8.5倍。

Conclusion: FedCC在保证高检测性能的同时大幅降低了计算和通信开销，为资源受限环境下的隐私保护型胎儿神经超声临床AI部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate localization of the corpus callosum (CC) in fetal ultrasound (US) images is crucial for the early identification of neurodevelopmental abnormalities. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intrinsic limitations of US imaging, including low contrast, speckle noise, and the considerable anatomical variability of the CC. We propose FedCC, a federated learning (FL)-based framework for CC localization in fetal US images, specifically designed for realistic multi-center and resource-constrained clinical settings without requiring data sharing. The framework integrates a frozen DINOv2 backbone with a lightweight YOLO-based detection head. To enable parameter-efficient adaptation, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are incorporated, allowing only a small subset of parameters to be optimized and exchanged among clients. This strategy substantially reduces both computational and communication overhead, making the framework suitable for low-resource environments. The proposed approach was evaluated on a multi-center dataset comprising 10,970 ultrasound frames acquired from 58 pregnant women during routine neurosonographic examinations across three clinical sites using heterogeneous imaging devices. The proposed framework achieved strong performance in the federated setting. In particular, the combination of DINOv2 and LoRA under the FedAvg strategy achieved an average mAP@50 of 0.857 and an F1-score of 0.803, outperforming both full fine-tuning and encoder-freezing baselines. Notably, the proposed approach reduced the number of trainable parameters to 2.9M compared with 24.4M in full fine-tuning, corresponding to an approximately 8.5$\times$ reduction in communication cost. These findings represent a promising step toward scalable, privacy-preserving, and clinically deployable AI systems for fetal neurosonography.

</details>


### [136] [Compressing What Matters: Neuron Importance Meets Data-Aware Low Rank Approximation for Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18284)
*Athanasios Ntovas,Alexandros Doumanoglou,Petros Drakoulis,Dimitris Zarpalas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合参数重要性和逐层功能等价性的SVD压缩方法，并设计了动态压缩率分配算法，在高压缩比下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数规模巨大，导致内存需求过高，限制了其在资源受限环境中的应用。现有SVD压缩方法要么从参数重要性角度出发，要么从逐层功能等价性角度出发，但两者未被有效结合；同时压缩率分配多采用均匀分配或计算昂贵的启发式搜索。

Method: 1) 将参数重要性和逐层功能等价性两种视角结合到单一目标中进行低秩近似；2) 提出一种增强且计算高效的动态压缩率分配算法，替代均匀分配或启发式搜索。

Result: 实验表明该方法与最先进方法表现相当或更优，尤其在高压缩比下效果显著。

Conclusion: 结合两种视角的SVD压缩方法配合动态压缩率分配，能够在高压缩比下有效平衡压缩误差与下游任务性能。

Abstract: To excel at their domain large language models are comprised of billions of parameters. Yet this comes at the cost of huge memory requirements restricting their applicability in resource-constrained environments. To address the problem of neural network (NN) compression Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) has played a key role as a fundamental component for matrix compression through decomposition. To minimize compression error and to maximize the efficacy of the compressed model on the downstream tasks previous works focused on low-rank approximation of the NN's weight matrices either from the perspective of parameter importance or per-layer functional equivalence. While previous works studied the aforementioned perspectives in isolation in this work we are investigating the effectiveness of an approach that combines ideas from these two perspectives in a single objective. In parallel to this an important aspect that affects the compression quality is the distribution of the compression rate across layers and NN parameters. Earlier works mostly considered distributing the compression rate uniformly across layers and network weights or relied on computationally expensive heuristic search. Contrary to them in this work we propose an enhanced and computationally efficient algorithm for dynamic compression rate allocation. Experimental results support the efficacy of the proposed approach which performs on par or substantially better than the previous state-of-the-art especially under high compression ratios.

</details>


### [137] [Edge-Efficient Transformer for End-to-End RF Spectrum Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18285)
*Zhifan Song,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos,Hassan Aboushady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出E-SpecFormer（Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer），一种用于端到端自动调制识别和隐蔽信道识别的边缘高效模型。核心创新是LiTAN（Linear Tanh Attention Network），一种去除了Softmax和LayerNorm的注意力机制，在降低复杂度的同时提升射频任务精度。模型提供四种可扩展变体（Nano, Small, Medium, Large）。在RadioML2018数据集上，Nano变体在SNR>0 dB条件下达到86.5%的平均调制识别准确率，在基于硬件木马的CC数据集上达到94.2%准确率，参数量少于10k，FPGA/CPU协同执行速度达92 μs/帧，以极低成本超越现有边缘模型。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调制识别（AMR）和隐蔽信道（CC）识别模型在边缘设备上部署时面临计算复杂度过高、参数量大、推理速度慢等问题，难以满足IoT设备对实时频谱智能的实时性和资源约束要求。

Method: 提出E-SpecFormer架构，其核心是LiTAN（Linear Tanh Attention Network）——一种摒弃Softmax和LayerNorm的线性注意力机制，在降低计算复杂度的同时提升射频任务精度。模型设计了四种可扩展变体（Nano, Small, Medium, Large），以适应不同硬件约束。采用FPGA/CPU协同执行架构加速推理。

Result: 在RadioML2018调制识别任务中，Nano变体在SNR>0 dB条件下达到86.5%平均准确率；在基于HT的CC数据集上达到94.2%准确率。参数量少于10k，FPGA/CPU协同推理速度达92 μs/帧，以极低成本超越现有最先进边缘模型。

Conclusion: E-SpecFormer被证明是一种边缘高效的实时频谱智能解决方案，适用于IoT设备，在极低参数量和快速推理的前提下实现了卓越的识别精度，为边缘部署的深度学习频谱感知提供了可行范式。

Abstract: We present E-SpecFormer (Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer) for end-to-end automatic modulation and covert channel (CC) recognition. We introduce LiTAN (Linear Tanh Attention Network), a Softmax- and LayerNorm-free attention mechanism that reduces complexity while increasing accuracy in RF tasks. E-SpecFormer is parameterized in four scalable variants (Nano, Small, Medium, Large) to accommodate diverse hardware constraints. Using the RadioML2018 dataset for modulation recognition, the Nano variant achieves 86.5% average accuracy for Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNRs)>0 dB, and on the hardware Trojan (HT)-based CC dataset it reaches 94.2% accuracy, both with fewer than 10k parameters and up to speed of 92 μs per frame on FPGA/CPU co-execution, surpassing state-of-the-art edge models at a fraction of their cost. These results establish E-SpecFormer as an edge-efficient solution for real-time spectrum intelligence on Internet of Things (IoT) devices. GitHub link to the repository: https://github.com/zsniko/E-SpecFormer.

</details>


### [138] [Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Runtime-Tunable Transit Signal Priority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18286)
*Philip-Roman Adam,Stefanie Schmidtner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种偏好条件式公交信号优先控制器，通过运行时调节偏好参数w实现公交优先与整体交通延误的灵活权衡，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习TSP方法通常优化固定奖励或标量化，缺乏应对时段变化或扰动条件下机构优先级变化的运行灵活性。

Method: 构建偏好条件策略π(a|s,w)，在最小/最大绿灯时间和过渡可行性约束下选择信号相位；基于IntersectionZoo实现约束信号控制/TSP封装，扩展场景生成以加入公交密度增强和时刻表插入。

Result: 单一学习条件策略跨运行时偏好生成平滑的经验权衡前沿，优于固定时间和基于规则的基线，在中等偏好设置下非公交外部性保持有限，但在高公交优先级权重下显著增加。

Conclusion: 偏好条件化方法使单模型支持运行时灵活调参，兼顾公交优先与总体交通延误，但极端偏好下外部性风险需注意。

Abstract: Transit signal priority (TSP) requires balancing competing objectives: reducing bus delay while limiting adverse impacts on non-bus traffic and avoiding extreme waits for a subset of vehicles. Existing reinforcement-learning (RL) approaches to TSP typically encode transit-aware features (e.g., occupancy and schedule deviation) but optimize a fixed reward or fixed scalarization, which limits operational flexibility when agency priorities change across time-of-day or disruption conditions. We present a preference-conditioned TSP controller, $π(a \mid s,w)$, that selects the next signal phase under minimum/maximum green and transition-feasibility constraints and can be tuned at runtime via a preference parameter $w$ to trade off bus-priority emphasis against overall traffic delay without retraining. We implement this on top of IntersectionZoo by introducing a constrained signal-control/TSP wrapper, and we extend scenario generation with bus-prevalence augmentation and timetable-based bus insertion to address sparse transit-priority events during training. Experiments against fixed-time control, a rule-based TSP overlay, and fixed-weight PPO specialists show that a single learned conditioned policy spans a smooth empirical trade-off frontier across runtime preferences, outperforms fixed-time and rule-based baselines, and maintains constraint feasibility, while tail-delay diagnostics reveal that non-bus externalities remain limited for moderate preference settings but can increase substantially under high bus-priority weights. The source code of this work is available at https://github.com/urbanAIthi/morl-tsp.

</details>


### [139] [BearingNAS: Obtaining In-Sensor Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems for Bearings Using a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18287)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Paolo Gastaldo,Antonio Frisoli,Rodolfo Zunino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BearingNAS，一种面向传感器内处理的硬件感知神经架构搜索框架，针对极端微预算（4-8KB RAM、16-32KB Flash）进行约束优化，无需GPU即可在笔记本电脑CPU上一小时内完成搜索，在ISPU上达到99.50%的诊断准确率，验证了将机器学习工作负载迁移至传感器封装内部的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轴承故障诊断依赖外部计算资源（如GPU）进行处理，成本高且延迟大。本文旨在将智能诊断能力直接迁移至传感器芯片内部，消除对外部硬件的依赖，实现低功耗、低成本、可规模化部署的传感器内处理方案。

Method: 将神经架构搜索建模为约束优化问题，目标为极端微预算（4-8KB RAM、16-32KB Flash）；提出轻量级无导数搜索策略，无需GPU；设计单数据流搜索空间，采用衰减卷积核增长公式防止参数爆炸；针对STMicroelectronics的MCU和ISPU三个目标平台进行优化。

Result: 在笔记本电脑CPU上运行，搜索收敛时间不到一小时；在CWRU轴承基准数据集上，针对LSM6DSO16IS ISPU的最优架构达到99.50%的诊断准确率。

Conclusion: BearingNAS验证了在传感器封装内部直接运行机器学习工作负载的可行性，为低成本、生产级轴承故障诊断提供了新路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces BearingNAS, a Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search (HW-NAS) framework designed to shift the intelligence directly onto the sensor die via in-sensor processing. BearingNAS frames the search as a constrained optimization problem targeting extreme micro-budgets (4 to 8 kiB of RAM and 16 to 32 kiB of Flash). To eliminate the reliance on expensive discrete GPUs, we propose a lightweight, derivative-free search strategy paired with a single data-flow search space that leverages a decaying kernel growth formulation to prevent parameter explosion. We evaluate our framework on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) bearing benchmark, optimizing architectures for three STMicroelectronics targets: two commodity microcontrollers and the LSM6DSO16IS Intelligent Sensor Processing Unit (ISPU). Running entirely on a laptop CPU, the search converges in less than an hour. The resulting best in-sensor architecture achieves a highly competitive diagnostic accuracy of 99.50\% on the ISPU. These results demonstrate the viability of shifting the machine learning workload inside the sensor package, enabling low-cost, production-scale bearing fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [140] [Multi-Timescale Latent-Action DRL for Joint Optimization in Edge-Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18288)
*Vo Phi Son,Van-Dinh Nguyen,Ngoc Hung Nguyen,Trinh Van Chien,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于两时间尺度多层级深度强化学习与潜在动作空间（2T-MDRL-LA）的联合服务放置、计算委托与功率控制框架，用于解决分层边缘云系统中因负载不均导致的端到端延迟问题，实现了近最优的延迟和资源利用率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分层边缘云系统中，动态任务到达和异构资源导致跨层负载不均，造成严重排队延迟和资源利用率低下，亟需一种联合优化方法来最小化平均端到端延迟。

Method: 将混合整数非凸NP-hard的JSCP问题分解为长期系统配置和短期资源分配两个子问题，提出基于变分自编码器压缩高维组合动作空间的两时间尺度多层级深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），联合优化服务放置、用户关联、计算委托、任务卸载和用户发射功率。

Result: 与分支定界解相比实现近最优性能；相比无计算委托方案，平均端到端延迟降低20.8%，资源利用率提升13%；收敛速度比传统PPO快约50%。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效适应动态网络条件，通过两时间尺度分解和潜在动作空间压缩实现了可扩展的优化求解，在延迟和资源利用率上均有显著提升。

Abstract: Load imbalance across edge and cloud layers degrades latency performance in hierarchical edge-cloud computing (HECC) systems under dynamic task arrivals and heterogeneous resources, leading to severe queuing delays and inefficient resource utilization. To address this challenge, we study a joint service placement, computational delegation, and power control (JSCP) problem to minimize the average end-to-end (e2e) latency. The resulting JSCP problem is a mixed-integer nonconvex and NP-hard optimization problem due to the strong coupling between discrete and continuous variables. To enable tractable optimization and stable system adaptation, we exploit the inherent difference in decision dynamics and decompose the problem into long-term system configuration and short-term resource allocation subproblems. Based on this formulation, we propose a two-timescale multi-layer deep reinforcement learning framework with a latent action space (2T-MDRL-LA) to jointly optimize service placement, user association, computational delegation, task offloading, and user transmit power. A latent action representation based on a variational autoencoder is introduced to efficiently compress the high-dimensional combinatorial action space. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively adapts to dynamic network conditions and achieves near-optimal performance compared to branch-and-bound solutions. It achieves up to a 20.8% reduction in average e2e latency and a 13% improvement in resource utilization over the scheme without the computational delegation, while converging approximately 50% faster than conventional proximal policy optimization.

</details>


### [141] [Towards Principled Continual Anomaly Detection: A Systematic Framework and Benchmark Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18289)
*Kamil Faber,Mateusz Smendowski,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对表格型数据连续异常检测（CAD）中任务定义随意、基准设计缺乏系统性的问题，提出一个可复现的基准场景构建框架，能从现有数据集自动发现候选任务、过滤不合适的任务，并推导有原理依据的任务顺序，最终基于三个网络安全数据集构建了五个基准场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在表格型连续异常检测中，任务边界很少被显式给出，任意的数据划分可能产生不可学习、冗余或过度可迁移的任务，掩盖真实的持续学习行为，亟需一个系统化的方法来设计可复现、有意义的基准场景。

Method: 提出一个系统化框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 从现有表格异常检测数据集中发现候选任务；(2) 过滤掉不合适的任务（如不可学习、冗余或过度可迁移的任务）；(3) 推导有原理依据的任务排序，以暴露多样化的持续学习动态。

Result: 基于三个大规模网络安全异常检测数据集，产出五个基准就绪的场景，包括单数据集和多数据集的连续异常检测设置。

Conclusion: 该框架为表格型CAD提供了可复现的基准设计方法论，解决了任务定义随意性和场景设计缺乏标准的问题，有助于更可靠地评估持续异常检测方法。

Abstract: Continual anomaly detection (CAD) studies how models can adapt to evolving data distributions while retaining performance on previously observed regimes. CAD benchmarks, however, depend critically on how tasks are defined, filtered, ordered, and validated. In tabular domains, task boundaries are rarely given, and arbitrary splits can create unlearnable, redundant, or overly transferable tasks that obscure genuine continual-learning behavior. To this end, we introduce a systematic framework for reproducible benchmark scenario design from existing tabular anomaly-detection datasets. The framework discovers candidate tasks, filters unsuitable tasks, and derives principled orderings that expose diverse dynamics. The framework allows us to deliver five benchmark-ready scenarios from three large-scale cybersecurity anomaly detection datasets, yielding both single-dataset and multi-dataset CAD settings.

</details>


### [142] [SechKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Hyperbolic Secant Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18290)
*Hoang-Thang Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SechKAN，一种基于双曲正割(sech)函数的KAN架构，通过光滑钟形基函数和1D线性变换减少参数，在函数拟合、PDE求解和图像分类任务上优于MLP和其他KAN变体，但运行时间略长于MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN在机器学习和科学计算中展现出有效性，但现有KAN变体存在参数过多或性能不足的问题，需要设计更高效的KAN架构。

Method: 采用双曲正割(sech)函数作为基函数，利用其光滑钟形、局部响应和稳定梯度的特性；通过1D线性变换减少参数量，使模型规模与MLP相当。

Result: 在函数拟合、PDE问题和MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100图像分类任务上，SechKAN优于MLP和其他KAN变体，参数量相似；运行时间优于其他KAN变体但略长于MLP。

Conclusion: SechKAN是一种高效且性能优越的KAN架构，在多个任务上表现优异，未来可进一步优化运行效率。

Abstract: In recent years, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have attracted increasing attention due to their effectiveness in machine learning and scientific computing tasks, offering a new paradigm for neural network design. In this paper, we present SechKAN, a KAN architecture based on hyperbolic secant (sech) functions. The hyperbolic secant basis is used for its smooth bell-shaped form, localized responses, and stable gradients. We employ 1D linear transformations to reduce the number of parameters, allowing SechKAN to remain comparable to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in model size. Experimental results indicate the effectiveness of SechKAN in function fitting, PDE problems, and image classification tasks on benchmark datasets, including MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100. SechKAN achieves superior performance compared to MLPs and other KAN variants while maintaining a similar number of parameters. However, its running time, while better than that of other KAN variants, is slightly longer than that of MLPs.

</details>


### [143] [Dual-domain fused LSTM modeling for efficient time-dependent reliability analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18291)
*Yixin Zhang,Mingyang Li,Zichao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双域融合长短期记忆网络（DDF-LSTM）模型，通过将时间无关随机变量嵌入LSTM初始隐状态并引入全连接层融合双域信息，结合改进的损失函数强调最小响应敏感性，实现了高效准确的结构时变可靠性分析，四个案例验证了其计算效率和预测精度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代理模型方法难以处理时间无关随机变量及其与时间相关随机过程的复杂交互，导致时变可靠性分析精度和效率不足。

Method: 提出DDF-LSTM模型：将时间无关变量嵌入LSTM初始隐状态，引入全连接层同时映射LSTM输出和时间无关变量到最终输出空间，并设计改进损失函数以增强对最小响应的敏感性。

Result: 四个案例验证了所提方法在计算效率和预测精度上的提升，训练后的DDF-LSTM能以最小计算成本通过蒙特卡洛仿真高效估计时变失效概率。

Conclusion: DDF-LSTM有效捕捉了随机变量、随机过程与极限状态函数时变行为之间的依赖关系，为时变可靠性分析提供了一种高效准确的新方法。

Abstract: Time-dependent reliability analysis is crucial for ensuring the long-term safety and performance of engineering systems under uncertainties. However, traditional surrogate model methods often struggle to incorporate time-independent random variables and capture their complex interactions with time-dependent stochastic processes. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a dual-domain fused long short-term memory (DDF-LSTM) model for efficient and accurate time-dependent reliability analysis. A novel network architecture is developed to jointly process information from both time-dependent and time-independent domains. Specifically, the time-independent variables are embedded into the initial hidden states, and a fully connected layer is introduced to map both LSTM outputs and time-independent variables into the final output space. Furthermore, an improved loss function is designed to emphasize the model's sensitivity to minimum responses, thereby improving the precision of failure probability estimation. The proposed method effectively captures the dependencies among random variables, stochastic processes, and the temporal behavior of limit state functions. Once trained, the DDF-LSTM model enables efficient Monte Carlo simulation to estimate time-dependent failure probabilities with minimal computational cost. Four case studies validate the proposed method's enhanced computational efficiency and predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [144] [Real-time optimal control with shallow recurrent decoder networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19302)
*Matteo Tomasetto,Francesco Braghin,J. Nathan Kutz,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SHRED-ROM的实时闭环控制策略，利用少量传感器读数对高维参数化动力学系统进行降阶建模，通过模仿专家示范实现高效分布式控制，并在潜在层面结合传感器预测器应对传感器故障，在密度控制和流体控制等三个高维案例中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优控制方法在处理高维时空动力学系统时，需要多次系统仿真，计算成本极高，难以实现跨场景的实时自适应控制。

Method: 利用SHRED-ROM（浅层循环解码器网络降阶模型）对高维参数化动力学进行降阶建模；在少量专家示范样本上训练模型以模仿专家行为；合成潜在层面的传感器预测器以闭合控制回路，应对传感器故障或延迟。

Result: 在三个高维案例（参数化密度控制、流体流动控制）中验证了所提策略的有效性，实现了实时闭环控制并缓解了维数灾难。

Conclusion: SHRED-ROM结合传感器预测器的框架能够有效解决高维参数化动力学系统的实时最优控制问题，在保证控制性能的同时显著降低计算负担。

Abstract: Controlling dynamical systems in real-time across multiple scenarios is critical to enabling adaptive control strategies, ensuring stability and efficiency. However, to tailor control actions in response to varying scenarios, traditional optimal control problems typically require several system simulations, which are often computationally demanding due to the high-dimensionality of the underlying spatio-temporal dynamics. In this work, we exploit SHallow REcurrent Decoder networks-based Reduced Order Modeling (SHRED-ROM) to synthesize a real-time closed-loop controller for high-dimensional and parametric dynamics, relying solely on limited state sensor readings. After training the model on a few optimal examples given by an expert demonstrator, SHRED-ROM mimics the expert behavior with effective distributed control actions in new scenarios, alleviating the curse of dimensionality. Moreover, a sensor forecaster is synthesized and used to close the loop at the latent level, thus efficiently mitigating possible sensor failures or delays. The performance of the proposed optimal control strategy is finally assessed on three challenging high-dimensional cases dealing with either parametric density control or fluid flow control.

</details>


### [145] [Reliability Scales Inversely: Bigger Models Compound Mistakes Faster via a Hidden Auto-Regressive Risk Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18292)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 随着语言模型规模扩大，模型回答的开头更准确但整体可靠性下降更快。本文发现了一种被缩放效应加剧的自回归风险残差——模型一旦承诺一个低概率token并将其作为既定事实条件化，就会产生雪崩效应。通过对比强同族oracle模型，本文揭示了知识差距缩小约6倍的同时知识退化增长11-39倍；在捏造事实时，模型主观不确定性迅速放松而oracle参考风险持续长达17倍，形成"自信但危险"的风险区间（14B模型下增加69%）；该区间具有因果性——固定KL的方差收缩可使网络验证的幻觉降低35-74%；且该模式结构性规避自我监控，仅依赖p_M的检测器在承载近4倍更多捏造的风险分支上触发率低约30%。更大模型更快地滚雪球式犯错，这是一种主导性、自我延续、因果且对模型自身不可见的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释（知识差距论）认为通过更多数据、检索或规模扩展可缓解幻觉，但本文发现随着模型规模增大，尽管知识确实增加，但一种被规模放大自回归风险残差导致可靠性急剧下降。作者旨在揭示这一被忽视的失败模式：模型一旦在低置信度位置做出承诺，后续生成会将其视为既定事实而产生雪崩效应。

Method: 定义每个位置的log概率差异δ = log p_M - log p_O（M为被测模型，O为更强同族oracle模型），其二阶矩可分解为bias²（KL散度的平方）和风险（Var[δ]）。通过对比不同规模模型，追踪知识差距（bias）与知识退化（risk）的比例变化；分析捏造点附近的熵H(p_M)与oracle参考风险的时间动态；进行on-policy、固定KL的方差收缩干预实验验证因果性；评估仅依赖p_M的检测器（如语义熵）在风险分支上的表现。

Result: (i) 规模扩展下知识差距下降约6倍，而知识退化增长11-39倍；(ii) 捏造时模型熵快速下降，但oracle参考风险持续长达17倍，形成自信但危险的风险区间，在14B模型上此区间连接前后捏造的概率增加69%；(iii) 干预实验（方差收缩）在三个模型家族上使网络验证的幻觉降低35-74%；(iv) 仅依赖p_M的检测器在承载近4倍更多捏造的风险分支上触发率低约30%（p<10^{-16}）。

Conclusion: 更大模型虽然学到更多知识，但会更快地以雪崩方式滚雪球式犯错。这种失败模式是主导性的（占大部分错误）、自我延续的（一旦发生难以纠正）、具有因果性的（干预可缓解）、且对模型自身不可见的（自我监控失效）。这揭示了扩展律的一个根本性权衡：规模买来能力却侵蚀可靠性，且这种侵蚀比能力增长更快。

Abstract: As language models scale, answers start truer but degrade faster: scaling buys capability but erodes reliability. The knowledge-gap account - more data, retrieval, or scale - misses an auto-regressive risk residual that scale sharpens: the model commits to a low-probability token, conditions on it as established, and snowballs.
